Chapter 351: You Call This the Tiger Fist?

Ji Yuan, to some extent, still possessed a sense of fairness.

Of the nine young warriors from back then, he had met Du Heng, Lu Chengfeng, Yan Fei, and Luo Ningshuang. Lan Ningke was also one of the nine young warriors, so it was only right that Ji Yuan went to meet him first.

Therefore, Ji Yuan deliberately set up a scheme to meet Lan Ningke beforehand. After all, there was still some past relationship between them. If this meeting made Ji Yuan like him, he might take some action. It was impossible for him to interfere and order Lu Shanjun to do restrain himself, but it might be an opportunity for Lan Ningke.

Well, it was no use now. Ji Yuan had already met Lan Ningke and quite disliked him. It was now up to Lan Ningke’s own fate.

Meanwhile, after Ji Yuan left, Lan Ningke and his entourage also departed, feeling somewhat annoyed.

He had originally been in a pretty good mood after receiving a beautiful piece of calligraphy, but now that it had turned into two lines of sarcastic remarks, it felt as disgusting as swallowing a fly.

The key point was that the calligraphy was indeed beautiful. Even now, he still felt reluctant to throw it away; it was practically useless.

“Master Lan, actually, we can break the characters down. Look at this column; although it looks smaller on paper when taken out individually, it has a much better meaning.”

The person next to him unfolded the paper, tried to find the right spot to cut it in the middle with a chopstick, and made a vague line.

“Okay, let’s do that.”

Lan Ningke said coldly, his gaze still wandering around. The others added on resentfully.

“If I ever run into that scholar again, I’ll definitely give him a good beating!”

“If this weren’t Duming Prefecture, but instead Dingyuan…”

“But we watched that person write these characters, so why are there a few extra?”

The group was puzzled and felt something was amiss. They had no interest in wandering around the city for the time being and decided to return to the inn.

On the street, two carriages had just arrived from the direction of the city gate. Because they had to give way to pedestrians, they moved slowly through the city, stopping frequently. The one driving the carriage at the front was Lu Chengfeng.

He seemed nonchalant at first, but his spirits lifted when he caught sight of three people passing by on the street.

Seemingly sensing the intense gaze, Lan Ningke also turned his head to look in that direction and saw the two approaching carriages and coachman.

“Do you recognize the person driving the carriage? He looks familiar.”

Lan Ningke asked the two people next to him. They both looked over and shook their heads.

“I’ve never seen him before.”

Lanning frowned and muttered to himself.

“Today has been really strange.”

Having not seen Lu Chengfeng for many years, Lan Ningke had almost forgotten what he looked like.

However, Lu Chengfeng in the carriage clearly could not say the same. He squinted and observed the wealthy man in fine clothes in the distance. As the carriage drew near, he tightened the reins and brought the carriage to a stop.

Lan Ningke also stopped in his tracks, and the two stared at each other for a moment, about ten feet apart.

Lu Chengfeng spoke lightly, with a hint of emotion.

“Lan Ningke, it’s been many years. How have you been?”

Lan Ningke was startled. This person not only looked familiar, but also knew him?

“Who are you?”

Lu Chengfeng was slightly taken aback. He had considered several possibilities, but he never expected that Lan Ningke would not recognize him.

He suddenly remembered visiting Mr. Ji at Ju’an Pavilion, wine in hand. Back then, he had lamented to Ji Yuan that he could hardly remember the names of his compatriots. But when he actually met Lan Ningke, all the memories came flooding back, yet the other party really didn’t recognize him.

“Hehe, hehehehe, hahahahaha…”

Lu Chengfeng suddenly laughed hysterically, which greatly displeased Lan Ningke and his followers. One of them angrily shouted.

“What are you laughing at?”

“No, no, no, I wasn’t laughing at you guys, I was laughing at myself, hahaha… It’s something to be happy about, knowing that I’m not so pathetic after all.”

Lu Chengfeng stopped laughing and clasped his hands in a bow towards Lan Ningke.

“My name is Lu Chengfeng. Greetings, Master Lan. I hope to have the opportunity to witness your skills at this martial arts gathering.”

With that, Lu Chengfeng said no more, and with a gentle flick of the reins, the carriage started moving again.

Lan Ningke frowned as he watched the carriage disappear into the distance, thoughts swirling for a moment, before he suddenly realized something.

“It was him?”

“Master Lan, do you recognize this Lu Chengfeng?”

Lan Ningke nodded and informed the people around him.

“This person is from Cloud Pavilion in Ji Province. Cloud Pavilion was quite famous back then. I traveled with him in my youth, but I didn’t recognize him after so long.”

Lan Ningke wanted to say a few more words, but suddenly sensed something and turned to look to the side in the direction of the city gate, where the carriage had just come from. But all he saw was a stream of people coming and going. He felt a brief moment of unease.

With one strange thing after another happening today, Lan Ningke really lost interest in wandering around and took his men straight back to the inn.

As evening approached, the daytime hustle and bustle began to subside, as shops closed and people went home. Lan Ningke, who had rested for half a day at the inn, had to prepare to go out again.

Although he had been feeling somewhat restless since he had returned to the inn, and he didn’t want to go out, he had made an appointment for dinner with someone this morning. The other person was an old acquaintance and a renowned figure in the martial arts world, so it wasn’t appropriate to break his promise.

“Knock knock knock… Master Lan, it’s time for us to go to Rengui Restaurant.”1

His companions knocked on his door outside to remind him, and Lan Ningke responded.

“Okay, I’ll be right out.”

A moment later, the three of them went downstairs, left the inn, and headed towards Rengui Restaurant.

The sun had already set, and although the streets were not yet completely dark, the surrounding colors appeared dim and yellowish, and there were not many pedestrians.

Lanterns had already been hung up at Rengu Restaurant, the lively sounds could be heard from afar, indicating that business was booming.

It was the day of the Duming Prefecture Martial Arts Tournament, so naturally Rengui Restaurant, being a famous restaurant, was full of martial artists and enthusiasts.

Upon arriving at the door, the waiter warmly invited Lan Ningke and his companions inside.

“Sir, do you have a reservation? We have a lot of customers today. If you don’t have a reservation, you may have to wait.”

“It was Warrior Jiang Meng who invited us here.”

Hearing these words, the water’s eyes lit up, and he quickly replied.

“Oh, you must be Master Lan! Please come upstairs, please follow me upstairs. Master Jiang has already arrived at the window seat on the second floor!”

The waiter enthusiastically led the three upstairs. On the second floor, Lu Shanjun sat near the stairwell, occupying a table by himself. When he saw Lan Ningke come up, he grinned, revealing a half-smile.

In Lu Shanjun’s vision, Lan Ningke was surrounded by a murderous aura and lingering resentment. This in itself was nothing special, as people in the martial arts world were naturally prone to killing intent, and fights were commonplace. However, Lan Ningke lacked that indescribable sense of virtue.

Seeing Lan Ningke and the others sit down by the window, Lu Shanjun picked up a piece of braised pork from the table and squinted at the window.

The other table had five people in total, including Lan Ningke and his two companions, all sitting around an eight-person table.

“Rest assured, Master Jiang, I, Lan Ninke, will definitely achieve a high ranking in this martial arts tournament, and I will certainly support you!”

The man surnamed Jiang had a muscular and had a stoic countenance, but his words and actions somewhat contradicted his appearance. He chuckled as he poured wine for Lan Ningke.

“With Master Lan’s words, I am even more confident. This time, the martial arts world of Ji Province is preparing to unite as one. Whoever gains the upper hand will reap great benefits. If I rise to power, I will naturally not forget Master Lan!”

“Haha, Master Jiang’s Tiger Fist Technique is unmatched in the entire Ji Province, and few can rival him even in Dazhen. Moreover, he is both intelligent and courageous. It would be unreasonable for you not to take the position!”

Similar scenes were rather common, and there were many people like Jiang Meng. Everyone knew that this martial arts tournament was important, and similar campaigning tactics were commonplace. But this alone would not be enough; in the end, the real test would lie in their skills.

“Hahahaha… Lan Ningke! Jiang Meng! You two despicable villains are indeed here!”

A furious shout suddenly came from the stairwell, followed by four men dressed in blue outfits, each carrying a weapon.

“Oh? Who are you guys?”

Lan Ningke squinted and asked, thinking that his uneasy mood today was probably related to this matter.

Jiang Meng sneered; no one would be happy to be insulted like this in public.

“Hahahaha… You two thieving dogs, I knew you would shamelessly come to participate in the martial arts tournament. I am Fan Tong. You two destroyed my family and left us to die. Today shall mark your death.”

“Oh dear sir, you can’t fight in the restaurant, we…”

“Get out of the way! We’ll pay for any damage!”

The man shoved aside the innkeeper who came to persuade him.

“Shiiing!”

“Shiiing!”

“Shiiing!”

“Shiiing!”

All four of them drew their knives and pointed them at the people by the window.

Lu Shanjun ate and drank alone, without getting up. Instead, he used his spiritual energy to enhance his senses and listen to the other sounds in the inn.

“Look, that fat guy over there is Iron Whip Master Lan Ningke, and sitting opposite him is River Tiger Jiang Meng. Neither of them are good people. Back then, the Fan family of Dingyuan Prefecture was forced to the brink of ruin, and they were instrumental in getting them there.”

“Shhh…be careful what you say!”

“Hmph, their enemies have come knocking, and I’m not allowed to speak up? Besides, this is Duming Prefecture. During the martial arts tournament, even Master Luo will be present. He won’t tolerate such scum of the martial arts world taking over. Why should I be afraid of them? The Fan family suffered a series of calamities back then. First, they lost the Sword Intent Inscription, and then they were forced by the masses to hand over their years of research. Afterwards, they took advantage of their misfortune. We martial artists despise those people!”

Lu Shanjun glanced at the table that was speaking. There sat three ordinary-looking men. They spoke with righteous indignation, but they kept their voices very low, obviously afraid of causing trouble.

“Prepare to die!”

“Die!”

Fan Tong and his companions roared, brandishing their swords and charging towards Lan Ningke and Jiang Meng. Seing their movements, Lan Ningke felt much relieved, realizing they were not top-tier experts.

“Rooar…”

Jiang Meng let out a loud roar that carried the aura of a ferocious beast. He flashed to the table, his hands forming claws, and crouched low as he slipped through the crowd, wildly swinging his claws.

“Clang!”

“Bang!”

“Riip…”

“Pfft…”

“Bang……”

One man, with just his bare claws, managed to block four weapons, blood splattering everywhere.

These four men, though skilled, were far inferior to their opponents. Even without Lan Ningke making a move, they couldn’t withstand over a dozen strikes before being either knocked away or seriously injured by the claws.

The ordinary onlookers had long since fled in terror, while the martial artists, though they didn’t flee, didn’t dare to make a move.

“Bang…”

Fan Tong was struck in the chest by a palm strike and flew backward, crashing into a pillar on the side. He fell to the ground and spat out a mouthful of foamy blood.

As he looked up and saw Jiang Meng approaching, he gave a bitter smile.

“Heh, heh… I knew long ago that I was no match for you, but I just wanted to force you to act, force you to use your internal energy. I’ve already…”

“Hmph, do you think I wouldn’t have noticed your little tricks with poison? We were just pretending to drink.”

As Jiang Meng spoke, Lan Ningke took out a porcelain bowl from his sleeve, which was filled with the poisoned wine that they were supposed to have drunk.

“Hahaha…”

Fan Tong was still laughing.

“So what? Your reputations are ruined. There are many people attending this martial arts tournament, and soon everyone will know what happened today. Want to be a martial arts master? Want to gain an advantage? Dream on! Hahahaha…”

Fan Tong knew his martial arts talent was limited, and even with the addition of poison, he might not have succeeded. He just wanted to annoy these two people so much that they couldn’t stand in the martial arts tournament. As for the future of the Fan family, he would leave it to someone with more potential.

“You’re courting death!”

Jiang Meng was truly enraged now, and with a swift motion of his tiger claws, he moved to strike Fan Tong on the head.

“Whoosh…”

A whooshing sound whistled through the air, and before Jiang Meng could hit Fan Tong, he reflexively dodged.

“Thud…”

A chopstick shot out and landed on the floor between Jiang Meng and Fan Tong, embedding itself several inches into the wood. Its end still trembled slightly.

“Who is it? Who dares to meddle in this?”

Jiang Meng roared. He glanced over to the side, in the direction of the stairwell. His gaze swept over the people at the tables there. Many avoided his gaze, but one person remained still.

“Interesting. You think you can call that the Tiger Fist?”

A voice laced with obvious amusement rang out. A man in a blue robe who looked like a scholar stood up from his seat.

仁 (rén): kernel/humane, 贵 (guì): expensive/precious/noble –> “Expensive Bites” Restaurant

Chapter 352: The Potential of Shamelessness

Lu Shanjun’s build didn’t seem like that of a strong and muscular man. He wasn’t exactly skinny, but he was certainly quite refined and seemed gentle.

However, this martial arts world was full of extraordinary individuals, and some mysterious martial arts did not discriminate by physical condition. The fact that the chopstick in the floor was still trembling was enough to prove that the person who stood up was an expert.

Hearing Lu Shanjun’s obviously sarcastic voice, Jiang Meng narrowed his eyes and looked him up and down.

“Who are you, sir? This man poisoned my friend and me, then attacked us with a blade. He has only himself to blame. It’s none of your business, is it?”

Lu Shanjun smiled.

“No, no, I actually don’t really care about this Fan guy. It’s just that the guy over there owes me a debt, and you happen to be with him, so you might as well help him pay it off.”

Lan Ningke snorted and stood up.

“Hmph, you’re talking about me? I don’t remember ever seeing you before, nor do I remember owing you any debt.”

Lu Shanjun didn’t speak. He flicked his sleeves left and right, and his clothes slowly changed color, from the previous blue robe to a light yellow. The black cloud pattern on the edge of his sleeves also extended upwards, almost like a floral pattern.

This scene astonished the surrounding martial artsists.

“Does this person know magic?”

“Or is he performing a magic trick?”

“I don’t know, it’s a bit eerie!”

“Let’s see how that Jiang and Lan handle this.”

…

The whispers of the bystanders carried a mixture of curiosity and schadenfreude, while Jiang Meng and Lan Ningke themselves felt increasingly pressured.

The color change of these clothes was so striking; there might have been poisonous powder on them. If he truly was some kind of supernatural expert, this could become quite troublesome.

At this moment, Lu Shanjun glanced at Jiang Meng, then his gaze swept towards Lan Ningke.

“Of course you don’t remember what I look like. Let me give you a hint so you know who I am. In the early spring of the year of Dingchou, in front of the Mountain God Temple on Niu Kui Mountain, I listened to the master’s teachings and spared your lives. We made an oath that, after you nine escaped with your lives, you vowed to aspire to be chivalrous heroes for the rest of your lives. In the future, I would personally come down the mountain to see if you had kept your promise. Lan Ningke, do you remember now?”

Lu Shanjun spoke slowly, but every word was like a heavy hammer blow to Lan Ningke’s heart. His eyes widened and grew bloodshot, his breathing picked up speed, and his face turned extremely pale.

He remembered. He remembered that night many years ago. He remembered that scene outside the Mountain God Temple on Niu Kui Mountain. Then he looked at the color of the scholar’s clothes.

‘This… this is…’

Lan Ningke was terrified and slowly backed away. Sometimes, memories once thought to be forgotten are in fact simply buried deep in the heart. At this moment, with the crisis approaching, everything came back at once.

“Brother Lan, who is this?”

Jiang Meng stared intently at Lu Shanjun. Seeing that the other party had actually explained things clearly, he thought of asking Lan Ningke, but the latter’s emotions were obviously not very stable.

“Brother Jiang, he might not be human…”

“What did you say?”

Lan Ninke was so terrified that has voice came out quietly in broken sentences.

“He said I might not be human!”

Lu Shanjun grinned. His fingers curled up one by one, forming a claw-like shape with bulging tissue, and raised his head to speak in a low but loud voice.

“I will also be using the Tiger Fist technique. Watch carefully!”

As soon as he finished speaking, Lu Shanjun opened his mouth and roared.

“Roar—”

Boom…

Everyone around instinctively covered their ears. The enormous roar made all the porcelain tableware in the restaurant to shake violently, and many even cracked.

Jiang Meng felt as if he had been hit on the head with a heavy hammer. He was completely stunned, and a buzzing sound filled his ears.

But though he was dazed, he could still watch through wide-open eyes as Lu Shanjun crouched down and rapidly approached, his shoulder tucked at a slant.

In a daze, he seemed to see a fierce tiger with piercing eyes rushing towards him as it pounced, its aura flowing through his very bones, like a tiger pouncing on its prey.

Jiang Meng tried to react, but his body felt extremely heavy. Everything around him slowed down for a moment, and before he could even raise his hand, in the blink of an eye, the shoulder slammed hard into his chest.

“Bang…”

“Crackackackack…”

With a series of crisp cracking sounds, Jiang Meng’s chest caved in, followed by a sharp pain in his left chest.

“Boom……”

The window on the second floor of Rengui Restaurant exploded, and a figure, followed by fragments and splinters of wood, smashed through the wooden wall of the restaurant, flew seven or eight feet, before crashing into the street.

Jiang Meng was still alive, trembling on the ground, blood gushing from his mouth, unable to speak, his hands and feet seemingly shattered and unable to move.

“Hah…uh…hah…uh… cough…”

His eyes were fixed on the second floor of the inn. Then he looked down at his chest, only to see a large, gaping hole.

His strong physique allowed him to hold on for a little longer before collapsing to the ground, dead.

On the second floor of the inn, Lu Shanjun held up a bloodstained hand, with a bloody heart still beating in his palm.

“Oh, so this person’s heart is still red after all.”

This move, “Black Tiger Claws the Heart,” was so brutal and shocking that everyone around felt their scalp tingle, yet they remained completely silent.

Lan Ningke was the first to react, but he lacked the courage to fight back. Without saying a word, he leapt out of the restaurant through the hole Jiang Meng had smashed, channeling his inner energy to its limit and fleeing frantically for his life.

What martial arts tournament, what status in the martial arts world—none of that mattered as much as his own life.

Disregarding everything else, Lan Ningke took off running at breakneck speed. His qinggong reached a record level of skill, truly making him as swift as the wind.

He had considered going to temples or similar places, but Lan Ningke immediately dismissed the idea. He didn’t dare to gamble and place his hopes on those clay sculptures.

Lan Ningke’s first thought was to escape to Luoxia Villa, where Luo Ling, a top figure even among innate masters, resided. Only he could stop this monster.

Moreover, even if Master Luo couldn’t stop it, going in that direction was definitely the right thing to do. Lan Ningke knew that “Cloud Pavilion” was also in that direction, and it was a significant territory within Duming Prefecture. Lu Chengfeng would definitely be there!

‘I can’t die, or if I do, I can’t die alone! Lu Chengfeng… Lu Chengfeng was involved too! He was involved back then!’

A silent roar raged in his heart. His face contorted with a mixture of fear and elation.

On the second floor of the restaurant, Lu Shanjun watched Lan Ningke frantically fleeing, but did not immediately follow. Instead, he walked to the wall with the gaping hole in it, looked down at the lifeless body of Jiang Meng outside, then at the silent crowd inside the restaurant. Finally, he looked down at Fan Tong, who was equally horrified, but whose eyes revealed a deep sense of pleasure.

“Hey hey hey… Aaah… Spare me!”

“Great warrior, spare me! Spare me!”

“We’re just Lan Ningke’s attendants…”

“We know nothing, we know nothing!”

“Thump thump thump…”

Lan Ningke’s two companions immediately knelt down, crying and kowtowing to Lu Shanjun.

However, Lu Shanjun completely ignored them, and casually tossed the still-beating heart in his hand. It landed with a thud right in front of Fan Tong.

“I could have spared Jiang Meng’s life, but I owe your Fan family a small favor. Since you want him dead so badly, then let him die.”

This statement was made abruptly and without any context, but Lu Shanjun didn’t seem to have any intention of fully explaining. He left the heart behind and leapt lightly out of the restaurant.

Only after Lu Shanjun did the people in the restaurant feel the color drain back into their faces, and finally dared to breathe again. The oppressive feeling just now was too strong, as if they were mere children standing alone in front of a tiger.

“Was that an Innate Realm master?”

“No doubt about it. He’s probably one of the most powerful cultivators even among Innate Realm masters!”

“So terrifying, so ruthless! He literally ripped the heart out of River Tiger Jiang Meng!”

“Hmph, now he’s dead, discarded like the corpse of a dead cat1 downstairs.”

“Does he have some history with the Fan family?”

“No doubt.”

“This is going to be interesting…”

Fan Tong stared blankly at the heart on the ground until it finally stopped beating. A martial arts expert came over to help him up.

“Master Fan, are you alright? Get up, get up!”

“Congratulations, Master Fan, on avenging your great grudge!”

“Indeed.”

…

In the quiet of the night, the loud noise of fighting naturally attracted attention. The underworld messengers arrived even before the officials, accompanied by a chilling wind.

The two nighttime patrol officials stopped abruptly beside Jiang Meng’s corpse, frowning as they looked at the large hole in Jiang Meng’s chest and then in the direction of Rengui Restaurant.

“The roar just now came from here!”

“Hmm.”

The two underworld messengers scanned their surroundings, but did not sense any evil aura at the moment. When they turned to look at Jiang Meng’s corpse, they saw a dazed new soul emerging from his body. On the other hand, Jiang Meng’s soul was entangled with evil karma and malevolent energy.

“Hmph, there really aren’t many good people in the martial world.”

“Let’s take him with us!”

One of the underworld messengers reached out and yanked Jiang Meng’s soul out of the corpse, striking it on the forehead with the hilt of his sword. Jiang Meng, stunned, followed behind them like a puppet.2

Although Jiang Meng wasn’t a local, since they had encountered him, they naturally could not tolerate having wandering ghost around.

“That roar was rather strange. Could it be that some evil spirit has infiltrated the city? We need to investigate further.”

“That’s right!”

After exchanging a few words, the two night patrol officials surveyed their surroundings. Amidst the pervasive yin energy, their gazes fell upon the yang energy of the martial artist who had been alive only moments before. A thin mist seemed to form before their eyes, and through the haze they saw the outline of Lan Ningke fleeing in panic.

“Over there!”

“Let’s go!”

The two nighttime patrol officials stepped forward, transforming into two blurry ghostly figures, and chased after Lan Ningke in the direction he had fled.

…

Lan Ningke couldn’t care less about consuming his True Qi or maintaining his fighting strength. He knew perfectly well that he was no match for a monster; any resistance meant death.

Fortunately, by desperately stimulating his potential, he broke through the limits of his qinggong skill and soon approached the Luoxia Villa.

“Master Luo, Master Luo Ling—I’m being hunted down by evil martial artists! I beg you, Master Luo Ling, to help me! Master Luo, save me—!

“Lu Chengfeng… Lu Chengfeng, come out quickly! Our enemies are here! Come and help me!”

Forget about pride and manners, Lan Ningke didn’t care about anything now. He channeled his inner energy and shouted whatever came to mind as he charged towards Luoxia Courtyard.

Because his nickname/title is River Tiger, so now he’s just a dead cat

Lit. “like a wooden chicken”

Chapter 353: Don’t Get Yourself Dirty

It was currently neither too early nor too late in the evening. Many families were currently having dinner, as was the case at Luoxia Villa.

This year’s martial arts gathering, also known as the Ji Province Martial Arts Tournament, was being held in Duming Prefecture. The second master of Luoxia Villa stayed behind, while Luo Ling and Luo Feng, as prominent figures of Luoxia Villa, had both arrived in Duming Prefecture and were currently having dinner in the villa.

“Master Luo, Master Luo Ling—I’m being hunted down by evil martial artists! I beg you, Master Luo Ling, to help me! Master Luo, save me—!”

Lan Ningke’s loud shout, delivered with all his might, carried far and wide. Luo Ling and Luo Feng to both stopped eating.

“Let’s go out and see who’s calling for help!”

“Okay.”

Before they had even finished their words, their seats were already empty. Both of them were extremely skilled in qinggong, and with a few quick taps of their toes, they had already leaped over the garden wall and landed right in front of Lan Ningke.

Lan Ningke shouted a few more times, then was immediately startled when he suddenly saw a figure flash by in front of him.

“Ah…”

Due to the intense stress and excessive physical exertion, he lost his balance in his haste stumbled, despite being a seasoned martial artist.

Luo Ling swiftly stepped forward and caught Lan Ningke by the chest. He looked at him.

“Was that you calling for help?”

“Master Luo, Master Luo! I’m so glad to see you! You must save me! A villain is chasing and killing fellow martial artists in broad daylight. Jiang Meng is dead, but he will definitely come after me! He will definitely come kill me!”

Lan Ningke frantically recounted the night’s events, then looked around and saw Lu Chengfeng and several Cloud Pavilion disciples running towards him from the other side of the street.

For some reason, Lan Ningke was even more excited to see Lu Chengfeng than Luo Ling and Luo Feng, and his face displayed an expression of morbid excitement.

“Hey, Lu Chengfeng, Lu Chengfeng! I’m here, come over here!”

Luo Feng frowned as he looked at Lan Ningke’s current state. He sensed that this person was a bit abnormal. Luo Ling did not continue to support Lan Ningke, letting go of his grip as soon as he had regained his balance.

Luo Feng had successfully broken through to the Innate Realm several years ago and was now an Innate Realm expert. With two Innate Realm experts from Luoxia Villa here, he naturally wouldn’t be afraid of any tricks.

Lu Chengfeng naturally had excellent agility. He used his qinggong to rapidly approach Lan Ningke and the others. At this moment, the servants from Luoxia Villa also came out with lanterns, illuminating the surroundings.

Lu Chengfeng cupped his hands in a respectful bow to Luo Ling and Luo Feng.

“Patriarch Luo, Third Master Luo!”

These two were true martial arts masters, people whose mere presence could shake the martial arts world of Ji Province. They were always to be treated with utmost respect whenever anyone encountered them.

“Hmm, he said your mutual enemy has come knocking?”

Luo Feng squinted at Lu Chengfeng and Lan Ningke. He had just overheard Lan Ningke’s words calling out to Lu Chengfeng, and then he mentioned something about a villain chasing down fellow martial artists. He was still not quite sure what was going on.

“An enemy?”

Lu Chengfeng looked at Lan Ningke, who looked like a stray dog, completely different from his demeanorduring the day.

“What enemy? Warrior Lan, might we have any common enemy?”

Lan Ningke’s facial muscles twitched.

“Yes! Hahaha, you’ve forgotten too, you’ve forgotten too, hahaha, we’re all the same, all the same! He’s coming, he’s coming!”

Luo Ling frowned and was about to speak when suddenly he sensed something, and turned to look at the end of the street.

Under the starlit night, a figure dressed in a pale yellow robe was slowly approaching, his steps light and without any trace of supernatural abilities, resembling an ordinary person.

“He’s here! He’s here! It’s him! He ripped Jiang Meng’s heart out!”

Lan Ningke suddenly grew agitated and shouted.

Lu Shanjun approached as if strolling leisurely, gazing at the entrance to Luoxia Villa.

“Oh? Two Innate Realm masters. I’ve heard tell that Innate Realm Masters are all exceptionally talented and have great perseverance. I didn’t expect to find two of them. Indeed, your blood and vital energy are vigorous, and your Yang energy is strong. Hmm, Lu Chengfeng is here too, that’s good!”

Luo Ling squinted at the newcomer. Although he couldn’t discern how skilled the newcomer’s movements were, his tone sounded quite arrogant, and some of his words were difficult to understand.

“Who are you? Did you kill River Tiger Jiang Meng? Why are you relentlessly pursuing Lan Ningke? If there is any misunderstanding, please consider the pride of Luoxia Villa, and let’s sit down and talk openly.”

Such polite words are perfectly appropriate for Luo Ling’s status; both his status and his fists carried significant weight.

“No need for such trouble.”

Seeing that Lan Ningke was deliberately dragging Lu Chengfeng into the mess, Lu Shanjun realized that this person had no bottom line, and no longer felt the need to hold back. After speaking, he approached Lan Ningke like a ghost.

“Stop!”

Luo Ling reacted with lightning speed, reaching out to throw a punch and swinging with his left arm in an attempt to force the intruder back. However, Lu Shanjun easily deflected the punch and open-palm strike with a single wave of a hand.

“Hmm?”

Luo Ling and Luo Feng were startled. The former had used the force of their momentum in the exchange to twist at the waist and shift position, flashing in front of Lan Ningke. At the same time, the latter had already leapt up, his figure fluttering in the air like a butterfly. As he flipped over, he struck with both hands from the top of the head to the bottom, his strikes covering Lu Shanjun’s body.

At the same moment, Luo Ling had already launched another flurry of blows and palm strikes. This time it was no longer a test, but a full-force attack.

“Whoosh… whoosh…”

The force of the fists and palm strikes of the two Innate Realm masters actually produced a whistling sound of wind.

“Slap!”

“Slap!”

“Bang!”

“Slap!”…

Lu Shanjun dodged, waved his hand, flicked his sleeve, and parried with an elbow…

In the blink of an eye, Lu Shanjun exchanged over ten blows with the two masters of the Luo family, each exchange louder than the last, even causing cracks to appear in the floor tiles beneath their feet.

He single-handedly held off two Innate Realm masters, one of whom was Luo Ling!

The scene before them astonished Lu Chengfeng and those around him. Only Lan Ningke showed little surprise. Now that he had caught his breath, he looked tense, his expression shifting between anger and uncertainty.

“Well played!”

Lu Shanjun said coldly, sweeping Luo Ling aside with a flick of his sleeve, and turned around the instant Luo Feng’s palm struck from behind, meeting the blow with a smile.

“Bang…”

“Crack…”

Luo Feng felt a surge of powerful force; his right arm bones cracked and tendons snapped. He tumbled four or five times in the air, landing and skidding backwards. When he finally regained his footing, one of his arms was hanging loosely to his side and trembling slightly.

“Third brother, are you alright?”

Luo Ling asked nervously. Luo Feng forced a reply.

“I’m alright!”

Lu Shanjun smiled.

“You two are quite good; you deserve to be called skilled.”

As he was about to advance again, Luo Ling suddenly erupted into motion, pushing his True Qi to its limit. His movements and attacks were three times more ferocious than before, frequently flashing around Lu Shanjun. His punches and kicks were like afterimages, and he seemed to be blocking his opponent all by himself.

However, people at Lu Chengfeng and Lan Ningke’s level could already see that Luo Ling was at a complete disadvantage, unable to even force him to move a few steps back.

“Master Luo, I’m coming to help you!”

Lu Chengfeng shouted. He circulated his True Qi to use qinggong, instantly closing in on Lu Shanjun with violent momentum.

“Haah!”

The shout contained a large amount of true energy, and the mountain-shaking punch brought out a whistling sound as if the air was being torn apart.

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Bang!”…

A single punch exploded, unleashing a continuous barrage of afterimages.

Since this person was so arrogant and hardly moved, he had become a target. Lu Chengfeng imagined him as the giant tree stump in Cloud Pavilion, and was preparing to uproot him with a storm of punches.

Luo Ling and Luo Feng hadn’t expected Lu Chengfeng to help much, but they never imagined he possessed such martial arts prowess.

Luo Feng, enduring the pain, returned to the battlefield, using his left hand to unleash an open-palm strike to assist. Lu Chengfeng’s attack was fierce, but since he was not an Innate Realm cultivator, recovering his True Qi was his biggest weakness. His attacks would weaken immediately after this set of punches ended.

However, Luo Ling’s confidence soared. He wrapped his arms with Innate Realm Qi, and poured his True Qi into his attacks recklessly, striving to quickly defeat his enemy.

But Lan Ningke’s heart sank. After Lu Chengfeng joined the attack, he noticed that the monster still had time to glance at him frequently, and the expression on his face was always a half-smile.

‘They can’t stop them! They can’t stop him!’

With his mind in disarray, Lan Ningke suddenly turned and ran, sprinting towards the street once more.

“Bang…”

He had only run a dozen steps when he suddenly bumped into something and fell to the ground with a thud. Looking up, he saw Lu Shanjun, dressed in a yellow robe with cloud patterns, wearing a fake smile.

“Where are you planning to run off to?”

On the other side, Luo Ling, Luo Feng, and Lu Chengfeng stared in astonishment, a strong sense of defeat rising within them. The enemy they had just been fighting had vanished from their sight in an instant and appeared dozens of steps away, as if he had been toying with them.

“Spare me, spare me… I, I promise I will always uphold justice and always prioritize righteousness. I will definitely keep promise. I will repent and reform. Oh, and the Fan family — I will return the money I owe them, I will help the Fan family restore their former glory… I will… I will apologize with thorns in my back… I will…”

Lan Ningke immediately begged for mercy and made promises, speaking strings of words, kneeling on the ground and kowtowing repeatedly until his forehead bled.

Lu Shanjun looked to Luo Ling and the other two.

“Did you hear that?”

Anyone with half a brain could understand a lot from that string of words: at the very least, it was clear that Lan Ningke was not a good person.

Lu Shanjun smiled and helped Lan Ningke to stand, as he was still trying to kowtow.

“Get up, get up, stop kowtowing.”

Looking at the pale-faced man standing in front of him, covered in sweat and grime, Lu Shanjun even stretched out his sleeve to wipe the blood, sweat, and dust from his forehead.

“Look at you, don’t get yourself dirty.”

These words had barely left his mouth before Lan Ningke relaxed his guard for just a moment.

“Roar…”

Lu Shanjun suddenly opened his mouth, and a phantom tiger head appeared.

“Aaaah—“

Amidst Lan Ningke’s panicked screams, Lu Shanjun swallowed him whole; everything happened so fast it seemed like an illusion.

Everyone else was stunned, but Lu Chengfeng froze instantly, because in that instant, the man who had swallowed Lan Ningke was already in front of him, no more than two arms’ lengths away.

He broke out into a cold sweat instantly, his back soaked. Lu Chengfeng vaguely realized who this person was.

Lu Shanjun smiled at Lu Chengfeng.

“Are you afraid?”

Lu Chengfeng suppressed the urge to run away and answered in a trembling voice.

“Of course I’m afraid!”

“I heard you’ve hardly left Yuchang County in recent years. What’s your opinion on our agreement?”

Lu Chengfeng knew this was a life-or-death moment. Although the pressure was intense, he had to speak his mind.

“Family comes first, then chivalry. My family in Cloud Pavilion is unstable and surrounded by dangers. In times like these, for me, the way of chivalry is to protect my family!”

Lu Shanjun’s eyes flickered thoughtfully, but he did not speak. Instead, he looked towards the end of the street.

“What kind of demon is committing this violence here?”

“You wretched creature, you’re courting death!”

Two loud shouts echoed from the end of the street and reached Lu Shanjun’s ears. It was clear that the monstrous aura that had just revealed had been detected by the Nighttime Patrol Officers.

“Tsk tsk tsk, I’ve gone too far. I don’t want to get into conflict with the underworld.”

Leaving behind these light words, Lu Shanjun leaped up, his figure disappearing into the distance in a flash.

Chapter 354: Master of the Lu Family

Lu Shanjun’s movements seemed as though he were fleeing, but he was quite fast. To Luo Ling and Luo Feng, it appeared as though he disappeared into the night in just a few flashes.

Lu Chengfeng stood there, panting, his face covered in cold sweat, feeling as though he had been granted a pardon.

“Whoosh…whoosh…”

A gust of cold wind swept by, and everyone present instinctively shivered. Although they could not see the underworld messengers passing by, they were jolted back to their senses by this gust of cold energy.

Luo Ling withdrew his gaze from the distance, exchanged a glance with Luo Feng, and then the two looked at Lu Chengfeng together.

“Nephew Lu, what happened just now…”

Lu Chengfeng took a deep breath and, quite relieved, bowed with cupped hands in a gesture of respect towards Luo Ling and Luo Feng.

“It’s a long story. If the two masters wish to hear it, I will tell you in detail, but it is not appropriate to divulge it to outsiders.”

Ultimately, since Luo Ningshuang was also involved, Lu Chengfeng felt it was best to inform the two masters.

Luo Feng nodded to his elder brother, then turned to face the servants in the courtyard.

“No one is allowed to spread what happened tonight, understand?”

Many of the servants were still reeling from the shock of the earlier clash between top experts. Upon hearing Luo Feng’s words, they reflexively agreed.

Half an hour later, in Luoxia Villa, Luo Ling, Luo Feng, and Lu Chengfeng took their seats one after another and dismissed all the servants.

“Third brother, how is your hand?”

Luo Feng flexed his right hand and frowned.

“Although it looks like a severe injury, the bones were fractured, not broken; the tissue is intact, and I have not lost any sensation in my hand. With my Innate Realm True Qi, my body has an extremely strong recovery ability. With the help of some ointment, it will naturally heal in less than two months.”

Luo Ling nodded. He personally picked up the teapot, poured tea, and served a cup to Luo Feng and Lu Chengfeng.

“Nephew Lu, your father and I are old friends. Let’s talk about what happened today.”

“Thank you, Master Luo.”

Lu Chengfeng thanked him, then spoke slowly after he had taken some time to recall the events that had transpired.

“I’m sure the two masters noticed before that that man’s strength was not so much due to his exceptional martial arts skills, but rather because of his incredibly strong physique. If you add that to the scene of Lan Ningke being devoured, it’s not hard to deduce that he was not actually human.”

Lu Chengfeng didn’t keep them in suspense. He sighed before continuing.

“If there are no unexpected factors, then this man was in fact a monster, and the debt he mentioned is indeed real…”

“A monster!?”

Although Luo Ling and Luo Feng had some suspicions, they were still taken aback when they heard this information.

“That’s right, he is a tiger from Niu Kui Mountain that has transformed into human form…”

Lu Chengfeng recounted the events of that year as he remembered them. Luo Ling and Luo Feng were both shocked and incredulous, but they had no choice but to believe the facts.

“So, even Ningshuang is in danger?”

“It’s very likely.”

Luo Feng flexed his right hand and frowned.

“But if such a monster intends to harm Ningshuang, we cannot stop it!”

Lu Chengfeng could only try his best to reassure them.

“Judging from the tiger spirit’s actions, he is very principled. Although we had this agreement, he is by no means a cruel and bloodthirsty individual. Otherwise, he would not have let me go. Junior Sister Luo married early and stayed at home to take care of her husband and children. She would not do anything evil. I don’t think the tiger spirit would harm her.”

“Alas, I hope so.”

Now that things had come to this point, this was all Luo Ling could say. But Luo Feng thought of something even more crucial.

“So, the blind man I met once at the inn was Mr. Ji?”

When Ji Yuan was mentioned, Lu Chengfeng showed genuine respect.

“That’s right, that was Mr. Ji. It is because of Mr. Ji that I am willing to believe that the tiger spirit wouldn’t harm people indiscriminately. This agreement applies both to the nine of us as well as to the tiger spirit; that monster also needs to cultivate the righteous path.”

“Then is it possible to find Mr. Ji?”

Hearing Luo Feng’s words, Lu Chengfeng shook his head.

“I am not at liberty to disclose Mr. Ji’s residence. Moreover, he travels all over the world and is not home most of the time, so it is impossible to find him in a short time.”

Luo Ling was still a little worried.

“Even so, we still can’t be sure that Ningshuang is alright. Third brother, you continue to stay at the manor here. I will go back to Luoxia Villa immediately to check on Ningshuang. If things go wrong, we’ll move her to the capital for temporary refuge.”

“Alright, eldest brother, you be careful too!”

The three masters of Luoxia Villa were quite close. Of the three, Luo Ling and Luo Feng each had two sons, while the second master only had one daughter, Luo Ningshuang. Everyone treated Luo Ningshuang like their own, and was no exaggeration to say that she was the apple of Luoxia Villa’s eye.

After exchanging a few words, Luo Ling immediately got up and headed towards Desheng Prefecture.

…

On the other side, Lu Shanjun spent some time using some tricks to shake off the two nighttime patrol officers. After wandering around outside the city for a while, he returned and re-entered from the other side.

Lu Shanjun actually agreed with Lu Chengfeng’s words to a certain extent. The so-called principle of chivalry should only extend to acts that could be done within one’s ability. In fact, he had already understood this tenet when he first met Luo Ningshuang.

As long as those nine people didn’t commit any heinous or immoral acts and lived their own lives honestly, nothing would happen to them. At most, they would be intimidated and feel self-conscious for the rest of their lives.

After returning to the prefectural city, Lu Shanjun concealed his presence and aura, then wandered through the streets and alleys to a slightly secluded inn. He easily reached the door of one of the rooms.

Inside was Fan Tong. Although he had attacked Jiang Meng and Lan Ningke with a blade that evening, the government of Duming Prefecture did not intervene.

They had only arrived at the scene primarily because the restaurant had reported the scuffle to the authorities. Fan Tong compensated the restaurant for its losses and reached a settlement. Since no one else reported the case, so long as it didn’t affect people’s livelihoods, the principle of “what happens in the martial world stays in the martial world” would naturally apply,1 and the government didn’t take any further action.

Therefore, Fan Tong now returned to the inn to rest.

After what had happened, Fan Tong couldn’t sleep at all. He didn’t know when the Fan family had become acquainted with such a powerful expert, and he couldn’t understand why this person hadn’t appeared when the Fan family was in trouble.

But no matter what, this was a good thing. The Fan family’s biggest enemies were Jiang Meng and Lan Ningke. As long as that expert could avenge the Fan family, the Fan family was willing to pay any price. Either way, they had nothing left to lose.

“I wonder if Lan Ningke is dead.”

Fan Tong murmured, drinking a cup of tea and muttering to himself.

“He’s dead.”

A detached voice came from outside the door, startling Fan Tong, before he suddenly grew overjoyed. He quickly got up and went to open the door, where he found Lu Shanjun standing in the hall.

“Benefactor, have you already killed Lan Ningke?”

“Yes, and there’s no trace of his remains.”

Lu Shanjun spoke these words casually, yet he possessed a convincing charisma that made Fan Tong extremely happy.

“Excellent, hahaha, excellent! Heaven’s justice is clear, and retribution is swift. These people will all eventually reap what they sow. By the way, I still don’t know your esteemed name, benefactor, and what your relationship is with my Fan family?”

Fan Tong excitedly clenched his fist and then bowed respectfully to Lu Shanjun in greeting.

Lu Shanjun entered the room, took a teacup, poured himself a cup of tea, and looked back at Fan Tong.

“My name is Lu Shanjun. I have no particularly close relationship with your Fan family, and I will not concern myself with the remaining grudges of your Fan family.”

Fan Tong’s smile froze, but he forced a smile and poured Lu Shanjun another cup of tea.

“Benefactor, you have already avenged the Fan family by eliminating Lan Ningke and Jiang Meng, so we naturally cannot ask for anything more.”

“Haha, you are quite sensible.”

Lu Shanjun smiled, thinking that this predestined connection would please his master greatly. He then sat down to continue speaking.

“In that case, I won’t be stingy. How about this: you can ask Lu Chengfeng for help in the future and ask him to take care of your Fan family. Just say it’s on my behalf; on behalf of Lu Shanjun.”

“Lu Chengfeng?”

Fan Tong was slightly puzzled. They both shared the surname Lu, so was he someone who was close with Senior Lu Shanjun?

Lu Shanjun, of course, was unaware of what Fan Tong was thinking, and spoke frankly.

“Lu Chengfeng is a member of the Lu family of Cloud Pavilion. The Jade Cloud Pavilion in this city that is also a Cloud Pavilion property. You can find him there. If you encounter any difficulties in the future, as long as they are reasonable, just go to him. His reputation will likely rise after your martial arts tournament.”

With these words, Lu Shanjun finished his tea and left the inn room. Fan Tong immediately went to see him off, but when he got outside, he found that there was no one in the corridor.

“Such terrifying skill in qinggong, it’s simply unbelievable!”

Overwhelmed with excitement, Fan Tong sighed and returned to his room. With the help of the Lu family of Cloud Pavilion, the Fan family might truly be able to turn their fortunes around. Most crucially, the Lu family actually possessed such an extraordinary individual who was not only talented in martial arts but also appeared remarkably youthful.

Of course, his true age was definitely much older than he appeared; he must have mastered the art of preserving his youth. At least, Fan Tong didn’t believe that he was really only in his twenties.

Fan Tong had heard that Cloud Pavilion had long since declined, so he naturally imagined a possibility: that the Lu family of Cloud Pavilion had a master who had been secretly been cultivating their martial arts elsewhere, unaware of the great changes that had befallen Cloud Pavilion. Now that he had returned, Cloud Pavilion would naturally rise again.

That must have been why he had said that Lu Chengfeng’s reputation would rise after this martial arts tournament.2

The more he thought about it, the more he grew convinced, and the more excited he became. Fan Tong swept away the gloom in his heart that had lingered for so many years, felt that the future of the Fan family was bright. At least, he no longer had to live in fear.

…

On the city wall of the prefecture’s capital, Ji Yuan, dressed in white robes, lay reclining on the eaves of the roof, his left arm supporting his head, looking out of the city. Lu Shanjun had just left moments ago.

At that moment, two blurry black shadows flashed, transforming into two underworld officials dressed in official robes and tall hats, standing on the city wall below. These were none other than the Left and Right Envoys of the Night Patrol of Duming Prefecture.

“Mr. Ji, the City God wishes to invite you to visit the underworld of Duming Prefecture. Would you be interested in going?”

Ji Yuan straightened up, slid down from the heights onto the city wall, and bowed to the two night patrol officers. The latter did not dare be negligent and hurriedly returned the bow.

“I will not bother you any longer. Please give my regards to the City God. Thank you both for your understanding regarding tonight’s events.”

Although he knew that the underworld was unlikely to catch Lu Shanjun, Ji Yuan was here, and sorting out the situation was only a matter of saying a few words, so there was no need to let the misunderstanding escalate.

“Making and keeping promises is only proper. We are not unreasonable. Mr. Ji, you are too kind.”

Hearing the Night Wanderer’s righteous words, Ji Yuan simply smiled. This matter was dependent on both the situation and the person. If he hadn’t been there, the monster’s attack might have been far more severe.

Without saying anything more, Ji Yuan nodded and then stepped into the air, leaving Duming Prefecture’s capital behind.

Having met Luo Ningshuang, Lu Chengfeng, and Lan Ningke, Lu Shanjun’s worldly perspective should have become much more comprehensive. As expected of Ji Yuan’s valued disciple, he didn’t need Ji Yuan to correct him at all. He had a good character and good judgment.
