Chapter 398: Just Call Him Master

“Even the Heavens wanted to take it away!?”

Taoist Qingsong stared wide-eyed at the scroll in his hand, remaining frozen in place for a long time.

“It’s time to clean up this mess.”

The Old Dragon spoke raising his hand and pointing at the table. Instantly, a thin stream of water appeared out of nowhere and gradually expanded, covering the entire table.

“Splash… splash…”

Leftovers, fish bones, and other debris rose into the sky with the whirlpool.

Taoist Qingsong and Taoist Qingyuan instinctively leaned back, fearing they would be swept into the swirling stream, but naturally, with the Old Dragon’s control, they were not harmed in the slightest.

After the whirlpool passed, not only did the scraps disappear, but the tableware and large iron pot on the table were also cleaned. Not a single pair of chopsticks was missing.

“Alright, since Mr. Ying helped with the washing and cleaning today, let’s move our things inside first so we can make room for tea!”

Ji Yuan said, then stood up to move the tableware. Seeing that he didn’t use any supernatural techniques, the others also lent a hand and carried the things back to the kitchen.

Half an hour later, a teapot and teacups were placed on the large table. Ji Yuan had finished the goji berries during the day, but this time there was something a little better: a rice bowl on the table, half filled with steaming rice crust.1

The stoves at Cloud Mountain Temple were double stoves connected in parallel, which was perfect for cooking dishes on one stove, and cooking rice and steaming food on the other. Today there were quite a few people to cook for, so they had made a lot of rice, resulting in a lot of crispy rice crust.

With a few crisp crunches, Ji Yuan crushed a small piece of rice crust in his mouth.

“Tsk tsk, crispy rice crust should be eaten like this, with a slightly browned color and a crunchy texture!”

Ji Yuan felt that tonight was the most comfortable and pleasant meal he had had recently.

“Haha, this is the first time in my life that I have drank tea with rice crust!”

Qin Zizhou smiled and picked up a piece of rice crust to chew on. When he saw the Old Dragon and Ji Yuan looking at him, he suddenly realized that he technically had only been alive now for a couple years, so he immediately corrected himself.

“This is the first time I’ve ever experienced this, even if I count my past life!”

“Hey, this way of drinking tea is quite unique. No one has ever invited me to do this before.”

As soon as the Old Dragon spoke, Ji Yuan couldn’t help but interrupt.

“Mr. Ying must be joking. Who would dare to invite you like this? You’d probably slap them across the face and they’d be dead.”

The Old Dragon pointed to himself.

“Am I really that bad-tempered?”

“Not at all. At least, you’re quite good-tempered in front of me.”

While the big shots were bickering on the side, Taoist Qingsong and his disciple simply sipped their tea quietly, occasionally offering a forced smile. They only spoke when it was their turn to speak, and didn’t dare to interrupt.

Of course, the main reason for their hesitance was that Taoist Qingsong had an important task tonight, and the more time passed, the more nervous he became.

After a long time had passed, Qin Zizhou looked at the sky and spoke to Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, it’s time.”

The Old Dragon and Ji Yuan stopped their joking banter. The latter nodded to Taoist Qingsong. Taoist Qingsong and Taoist Qingyuan immediately stood up and ran towards the main hall of the Taoist temple.

Then the two Taoist priests bowed together to the star map in the main hall before strolling inside.

Qi Wen stood beneath the star chart with his legs planted firmly, and his hands clasped together in front of his abdomen. Qi Xuan took two running steps forward and leapt, landing squarely on Qi Wen’s hands. At that moment, Qi Wen suddenly raised his hands and pushed.

Using the force of this lift, combined with Qi Xuan’s own strength, the latter leaped onto the beam from which the star map hung. He swept his arm across the top of the banner and lifted it, causing the entire large star chart to fall down alongside him.

A moment later, the two Taoist priests solemnly carried the star map to the courtyard and stood before the table. Ji Yuan and the others had already left their positions and were standing there in front of them.

Ji Yuan stepped forward, bowed respectfully to the star map in the hands of the two Taoists, and then pointed at it.

Instantly, the star chart left the hands of the two Taoist priests and slowly rose, hovering horizontally three zhang above the ground. At the same time, Taoist Qingsong took out the scroll “The Transformation of Heaven and Earth” and slowly unfurled it.

Almost simultaneously as it unfurled, it removed itself from Taoist Qingsong’s grasp and floated to a position about two zhang above the ground, just below the star chart.

“We cordially invite Master Taoist Qingsong, Master of Cloud Mountain Temple’s sect, to stand beneath the Star Banner and the scroll of methods!”

Ji Yuan announced loudly. His unique Taoist aura created a mysterious and unpredictably sense of ritual, as if a vast yet elusive rhythm were echoing throughout Yanxia peak, and even the entire Yun Mountain.

Taoist Qingsong took a deep breath and, slightly stiff with excitement, strode until he was right underneath “The Creation of Heaven and Earth.” Looking up, he discovered that the scroll had faintly glowing patterns that were identical to the star chart, while the star chart above was already shining brightly.

At this moment, Ji Yuan activated his spiritual power and unleashed the true power of the “Transformation of Heaven and Earth” for the first time.

In an instant, before the eyes of the Old Dragon, Qin Zizhou, and the two Taoists, endless brilliant light shone around them, as if countless stars had suddenly appeared, as if the sun, moon and stars were falling from the sky.

The starlight in the sky fell like rain, constantly gathering at the Yun Mountain and Yanxia Peak, landing on the Star Banner, and then, through the “Transformation of Heaven and Earth,” it separated into strands that flowed to Taoist Qingsong.

In that miraculous moment, Taoist Qingsong suddenly deeply understood “The Transformation of Heaven and Earth.” He even felt the connection between the mountains and rivers and the endless starry sky in the heavens.

In a daze, Taoist Qingsong felt as if he were walking among the stars. They seemed within reach. Some were solitary, others were interconnected, each displaying layers of different powers that echoed the earth and the mountains.

On this land, mountains and rivers gave life and transformed. All things were born and all things died. Animals reproduced, mortals lived and breathed, immortals, demons, and spirits cultivated and comprehended the Tao…

Experiencing this wondrous phenomenon, Taoist Qingsong’s mind did not become unsettled; instead, it gave rise to a strong sense of awe for heaven and earth.

It was at this moment, which seemed both illusory and real, that Taoist Qingsong vaguely saw a cluster of starlight gathering around him. He subconsciously reached out and caught the falling scroll. A faint burning sensation came, but Ji Yuan had instructed him not to let go no matter what happened.

The burning sensation gradually intensified, accompanied by a tingling sensation, which then transformed into a stinging pain.

“Boom…”

A massive thundercloud suddenly materialized amidst the starlight overhead, with raging purple-gold lightning converging at its center, its power seemingly capable of annihilating everything.

For some reason, Taoist Qingsong knew that the lightning was targeting him — or rather, it was targeting the scroll in his hand. A surge of fear gripped him, and he nearly dropped the scroll to flee for his life.

Qi Xuan recalled what the Old Dragon had said earlier, and suddenly realized that he was fighting against Heaven and Earth for this supernatural scroll.

But Ji Yuan’s words kept echoing in his mind:

“Taoist Qingsong, no matter what happens, you must not let go. Once you do, the Wondrous Method of Heaven and Earth will no longer be yours to control!”

Qi Xuan clenched his fists tightly, beads of sweat seeping from his body. He gritted his teeth and remained motionless, not daring to close his eyes.

‘Don’t let go, don’t run away, it’s all an illusion, all an illusion!’

“Crack… Rumble…”

The overwhelming power of the heavens descended, and all the light in the sky was obscured by the lightning. The Taoist Qingsong was so frightened that his heart stopped, and he fainted where he stood.

Outside, the downward force of the celestial energy gradually slowed, and the surrounding strange phenomena also gradually weakened, before slowly dissipating.

Qi Wen was so frightened that he fell to the ground, panting heavily as he looked at his master, his legs weak, unable to stand. Ji Yuan and the others stood solemnly in place.

The star banner in the sky shone brightly. Although the light was slowly being drawn back into the banner, the process was extremely slow. With a flick of Ji Yuan’s finger, the star banner circled Cloud Mountain Temple and Yanxia Peak three times in the air before flying back to the main hall of the Taoist temple, hanging itself back on the beam.

Qin Zizhou approached Qi Xuan, who was staring at the sky with his eyes wide open, stiff and motionless. He noticed that Qi Xuan’s right hand tightly gripped the “Wondrous Methods of Heaven and Earth.”

“No pulse, his heartbeat has stopped, his breathing stopped!”

“Master! Master is dead? Master!”

Qi Wen, who had just been feeling weak and powerless, struggled to his feet almost immediately, wanting to rush to Qi Xuan’s side, but was immediately pulled back by the Old Dragon, who had taken a step to his side.

“Don’t go over there! Your master isn’t dead yet. Hmph, with the three of us here, whether it’s someone who wants your master dead or your master himself who wants to die, it won’t be that easy!”

Ji Yuan also took two steps closer to Taoist Qingsong, standing slightly in front of Qin Zizhou, and extended a finger. A wisp of fresh vital energy gathered at its tip.

“A withered tree returns in spring, heaven and earth bring forth new life!”2

Ji Yuan touched Taoist Qingsong’s forehead with one finger, and the pure vital energy rippled through his body in waves.

Then, Qi Xuan’s open eyes regained their light.

“Badump… badump… badump…”

A strong heartbeat could be faintly heard, and his pulse returned.

After a long while, Taoist Qingsong regained consciousness. The first thing he did was look down at his right hand. Seeing that he was still holding the scroll, he breathed a long sigh of relief, fearing that he had chosen to run away at the last moment.

The next moment, a profound understanding of the wonders of heaven and earth arose in his heart. He realized that a connection had been established between himself, the scroll, and the star banner. Qi Xuan tentatively tossed the scroll up in the air, and the scroll immediately turned into a beam of light that shot toward the main hall of the Taoist temple. Then it automatically unfurled and “merged” into the star banner.

Taoist Qingsong composed himself, turned around, and bowed towards the main hall. Qi Wen followed suit, and together they bowed to Ji Yuan and the others.

“Thank you all for your help, thank you!”

“Alright, the Transformation of Heaven and Earth is a treasure. You have finally gained its approval and used it to comprehend the mysteries of heaven and earth. Although you still need to cultivate, there will be far fewer problems with your understanding. You can now truly be called the head of the Cloud Mountain Temple Taoist sect.”

Ji Yuan spoke. He then felt a little thirsty, and took the lead in sitting back at the table to pour himself some tea. With him taking the lead, the group quickly sat back down at the table, and the atmosphere became more relaxed than before.

The Old Dragon insisted that Cloud Mountain Temple should also have its own barriers, but Ji Yuan said that it was unnecessary, at least for now. Qin Zizhou chuckled to the side, while the two Taoists were secretly excited.

Somehow the conversation veered back to the matter of divining the “Sword Intent Inscription” they had performed at the last visit. When Ji Yuan took out the “Sword Intent Inscription” to show everyone, a bunch of chattering little characters jumped out.

They kept calling him “Master” over and over, which amazed everyone watching.

Two days later, at dawn, only two Taoist priests and two martens remained at Cloud Mountain Temple. As for Qin Zizhou, he had departed with Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon, and it was unclear how long he would be gone.

Qi Xuan and Qi Wen sat cross-legged in the main hall of the Taoist temple, cultivating. The two little martens also sat on two separate futons, seemingly imitating their cultivation.

After morning meditation, Qi Wen suddenly asked a question.

“Master, you are the sect master, so what about Grandpa Qin and Mr. Ji? Should we hang up the portraits you painted?”

Qi Xuan frowned and thought for a moment.

“It’s best not to hang Mr. Ji and Lord Qin’s portraits outside casually for now. We can find a place to hang them later if we have some kind of protective barrier or a sacred land or something.”

“As for titles, Lord Qin should be the Wandering God of our Cloud Mountain Temple’s Taoist sect. As for Mr. Ji…”

Qi Xuan pondered for a while, and realized that even if Cloud Mountain Temple grew stronger in the future, given Mr. Ji’s personality, he would definitely not appreciate everyone calling him “the Founding Master Transmitting the Tao.”

Suddenly, the name those small characters use a few days ago flashed into his mind. Back then, those calligraphy spirits used that name haphazardly, but Mr. Ji did not seem to mind it at all.

“Clap!”

Qi Xuan clapped his hands.

“I’ve got it! In the future, if any disciples are accepted into Cloud Mountain Temple’s inner circle, tell them that the one with the character ‘Ji’ on his portrait is the ‘Master’!”

The crispy part of the rice that is left at the bottom of the pot

Couplet of 4/4 characters

Chapter 399: Planting Barriers Around Yun Mountain

“Master?”

Qi Wen repeated. He couldn’t help but recall those chattering calligraphy spirits from before. That night, when the “Sword Intent Inscription” had been unfurled, Cloud Mountain Temple had been more lively than ever. Thinking about it now still made him feel quite amused.

“Yes, just call him Master. That’s all. There’s no need to go into the details.”

Taoist Qingsong was quite satisfied with his idea, and judging from Qi Wen’s expression, his disciple seemed to agree. However, after glancing up at the star chart in the main hall, he solemnly addressed Qi Wen.

“Cloud Mountain Temple is now a Taoist sect, and we are now considered important figures within the temple. In the future, if we want to carry forward the Taoist tradition of Cloud Mountain Temple, we need to have solid cultivation. Therefore, we must practice diligently from now on!”

“Of course! By the way, Master, will you still be setting up your fortune-telling stall in the future?”

“Uh…”

Taoist Qingsong was speechless for a moment. He realized that as his cultivation level increased in the future, he would no longer be afraid of ordinary punches and kicks, or perhaps even the punches and kicks of warriors from the martial arts world.

“Well, cultivation is cultivation, and hobbies are hobbies. Mr. Ji has so many hobbies, and they don’t interfere with his cultivation.”

Seeing his master like this, Qi Wen knew that his master probably would never change in this lifetime.

The two little martens had finished their cultivation and got off the futon. They looked up at the star banner, squeaked twice at Qi Xuan and Qi Wen, and then darted out of the main hall and out of the temple, disappearing into the mountains.

…

At Cloud Mountain Temple, the master, disciple, and the two little martens were all engaged in cultivation, or chatting idly. Meanwhile, Ji Yuan, the Old Dragon, and Qin Zizhou naturally also had their own matters to attend to.

The three of them hadn’t actually gone far, merely flying around the Yun Mountain Range. Compared to Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon’s ability to ride the wind and tread the clouds, Qin Zizhou didn’t know such supernatural techniques yet. However, he was still a potential Wandering God, so he could use the power of the celestial heavens to travel through the sky. However, his speed was still slower than normal flight, so Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon carried him.

Nevertheless, neither the Old Dragon nor Ji Yuan ever looked down on Qin Zizhou. Even the Old Dragon considered him an equal. Previously, he had admired Qin Zizhou more for his character, but now he looked forward to his future abilities.

With Cloud Mountain Temple as his support, Qin Zizhou, who originally possessed a pure Yang body, had already cemented his destiny. If there had been a 30% chance of achieving his goals when he had first been brought here, it had now soared to 60%.

As they flew over Maoqian Town, the Old Dragon pointed to a certain spot below.

“Is that where the Personal God1 resides?”

Ji Yuan glanced at the Old Dragon and corrected him.

“That man’s name is Huang Xingye. To be precise, his human body and the personal god in his spirit are one.”

“Hehe, it’s all the same. How many years does a mortal live? Huang Xingye was already quite old back then, and he didn’t have many years left to live. Sooner or later, he would become a Personal God in a human body. Such a god is extremely rare. If the Personal God could escape into the world to find its own opportunities after Huang Xingye’s death, that would be better. But if he were to scatter along with Huang Xingye’s spirit after death, that would be a great pity!”

The Old Dragon was clearly implying something, and he believed that Ji Yuan must have already made plans. Ji Yuan would not deny this assumption in front of his two companion. The more hidden cards he had, the better.

“Indeed, that would be too much of a pity. Back then, I left an Edict for the local Land God of Maoqian Town, and asked him to take care of Huang Xingye. In addition, I also used spiritual techniques to conceal the faint supernatural phenomena on Huang Xingye’s body. Taoist Qingsong calculated that this person would die peacefully of old age.”

Even the Old Dragon had to admire Qi Xuan’s fortune-telling skills. Although he was a mortal, and his divinations were subject to many constraints and prerequisites, his predictions were truly accurate. He remembered that Ji Yuan had come to Qi Xuan to try his hand at finding those calligraphy spirits, and sure enough, Qi Xuan did so with ease.

“Let’s go!”

As soon as Ji Yuan finished speaking, the three of them rode the clouds toward Maoqian Town, and soon landed not far from the Land God Temple.

It was still early in the day, but almost everyone in Maoqian Town was a farmer, so they got up very early. There were already quite a few people coming and going along the roads in town, but few people stopped by the Land God Temple.

Ji Yuan and his two companions would stand out in a crowd individually, let alone grouped together. One was elegant and refined in a white robe, another had white eyebrows, a trailing white beard, and a long robe, while the third dressed in luxurious clothing and radiated an aura of nobility.

The three walked together toward the local temple. They should have attracted considerable attention from those around them, but at this moment it seemed that no one paid them any mind.

A peasant woman, carrying a three- or four-year-old child, had just emerged from the local temple after offering incense. She didn’t pay much attention to Ji Yuan and his two companions, who were now almost next to her. However, the child in her arms was immediately drawn to the three men, especially Qin Zizhou’s white beard that reached his chest.

The three men possessed a calm and refreshing aura. the child, unfazed by these strangers, even reached out to grab Qin Zizhou’s beard.

“Grandpa, may I touch your beard?”

“Oh no, no, that won’t do! If you pluck my beard, it will hurt!”

Qin Zizhou pretended to grab his beard to avoid it, but he still “accidentally” let the child’s chubby little hand brush against his beard as they passed each other, the little hand stroking his beard as they passed.

“Hmm? Child, who are you talking to?”

The farmer’s wife paused, straightened her child, and looked back, but didn’t notice the “grandpa.”

The child pointed in that direction and spoke vaguely.

“There, there, the beard, that long beard, that long-bearded grandpa!”

“A grandpa with a long beard?”

The woman looked behind her, wondered if her child had seen the local Land God.

The farmers in Maoqian Town, like those in other parts of Dazhen, were generally a superstitious bunch. They also believed that children could see things that ordinary people could not, so the child’s claims seemed plausible. She quickly picked up her child and bowed in the direction of the Land God Temple.

There weren’t many worshippers in the local temple to begin with, and now only the temple steward was present, resting on a bamboo chair. Although he was called the temple steward, in this kind of small temple, he was actually just a knowledgeable elderly neighbor who knew a thing or two about managing the place.

As Ji Yuan, the Old Dragon, and Qin Zizhou stepped into the courtyard of the Land God Temple, the temple steward grew drowsy and fell asleep in his chair within moments.

Upon reaching the statue of the Land God, they noticed a considerable number of offerings on the altar, along with an ever-burning lamp designed to withstand the wind, and two jars of sesame oil beside it.

“Please, Land God of Maoqian Town, I seek an audience.”

It wasn’t a critical moment, so there was no need to use a brutal method like Spirit Binding. Ji Yuan calmly imbued Taoist spiritual energy into his calm voice which penetrated the statue, reaching the ears of the local Land God.

After waiting for about ten breaths, the Land God emerged from the ground with a wisp of smoke. He looked up and saw Ji Yuan and his two companions. Startled, he quickly bowed and paid his respects.

“The local Land God of Maoqian Town greets the three venerable immortals!”

The local Land God recognized Ji Yuan. The undisguised dragon aura emanating from the Old Dragon, coupled with the events that had previously occurred in this region, suggested that he might be the Dragon Lord of the Tongtian River. The white-haired, bearded man standing side by side with the two was definitely no ordinary man, either. He was likely also at the level where his being would be qualified with the “True” rank.

“No need for formalities, Land God. Although looking after Huang Xingye is not a difficult task, it still requires decades of careful management and is far from simple. Today, I’ve come to bestow upon you a great fortune!”

Ji Yuan’s direct approach only stirred up the already unsettled emotions of the Land God of Maoqian Town.

“You’ve only been the local Land God of Maoqian Town for a few years, and you were originally an earth spirit. You likely have not yet been recognized by the City God of this region, correct?”

The Land God dared not lie and spoke frankly.

“Because of what happened at the Huang residence last time, the City God of Le County and the City God of Changchuan Prefecture have already acknowledged my position as the Land God, and both have endorsed me in the underworld.”

“Haha, that’s quite fast!”

The Old Dragon chuckled, not looking at the Land God or Ji Yuan, but rather at Qin Zizhou, who simply stroked his beard and smiled, before speaking leisurely.

“Then, Land God, if you were given a choice, to choose between continuing your cultivation in Changchuan Prefecture, under the protection of the prefectural and county city gods, and the other were to come under my jurisdiction in the future, what would you choose?”

The Land God spoke without even thinking, without needing to consider anything.

“This humble deity wishes to submit to the immortal’s jurisdiction!”

What a joke! When the immortal arrived, he said he was bestowing upon him a great opportunity. Wasn’t he obviously instructing him on how to choose?

After speaking, he cautiously glanced at Qin Zizhou.

“May I ask who this venerable immortal is?”

“I am Qin Zizhou. I have no great reputation or high status. You may call me Lord Qin for now.”

“Oh, Lord Qin!”

The Land God quickly bowed again.

Ji Yuan thought for a moment, then took out a small stack of glittering gold coins from his sleeve and handed them directly to the Earth God.

“Take it, they will be useful. With your spiritual power, that edict might not work smoothly at times, but these coins will make it much easier to manage.”

The Land God’s eyes widened when he saw the spiritual coins. He carefully held them in both hands, repeatedly expressing his gratitude. He knew a little about spiritual coins, but these were simply treasures. He could feel the spiritual energy contained within as soon as he held them, and his heart was filled with a surge of excitement.

“Hey! When did Mr. Ji come up with this again? Can I have one to play with?”

The Old Dragon’s childlike spirit rekindled, and Ji Yuan simply tossed him five coins to play with.

“By the way, Immortal, now that I am under your command, is there any ceremony or ritual required?”

Qin Zizhou smiled.

“Just remember your decision. There’s no need for all that now. As for the underworld, there’s no need to inform them. Just do what you need to do, and you’ll find out when the time comes.”

“Yes! This humble deity shall obey the decree!”

In fact, rather than saying that the Land God was now under Qin Zizhou’s control, it would be more accurate to say that he would merely be asked to help look after the gates of Cloud Mountain Temple in the future. However, his future prospects were definitely brighter now than they had been when he had been the Land God.

After bidding farewell to the local deity and leaving the temple, the three went to the Huang residence to visit Huang Xingye. They then circled Yun Mountain once before landing in a suitable spot. The Old Dragon gathered the water all around them, Ji Yuan picked up his brush to write some barrier techniques, and Qin Zizhou guided the power of stars downwards.

The three worked together to plant barriers throughout Cloud Mountain.

Refer to chapter 161-167

Chapter 400: The Son Doesn’t Recognize Him

Because the sunlight and Yang energy were too strong during the day, the best time to harmonize Yin and Yang was at night, and it had to be around the same time as Taoist Qingsong’s initiation.

So by the time everything had been sorted out, several days had already passed.

One night early in the eleventh lunar month, Ji Yuan put his pen away, having finished writing a floating Edict. Qin Zizhou, meanwhile had not yet stopped moving, and the starlight was still falling like rain.

The Old Dragon beside him had also stopped, and stood beside Ji Yuan. He looked at the characters shimmering in the ripples of water elemental energy, within a radius of about ten zhang. A large amount of starlight converged, as dazzling as a galaxy. The falling starlight seemed to be expanding this galaxy of water energy, but in reality, the radius of the range did not increase; it was merely a mental illusion.

“Mr. Ji, you said this is essentially the same Water Element Gathering Array that you received from me, with only minor modifications. But to me, those minor modifications are quite significant!”

The Old Dragon watched as Qin Zizhou seemed to merge into the starlight, continuously amplifying the starlight within the “Spiritual River,” and gradually obscuring the already radiant Edict and incantation texts. He couldn’t help but sigh in admiration.

“Actually, the modifications are not that significant. Although Mr. Ying’s original Water Element Gathering Array seems simple, it possesses a profound and wondrous structure. I merely used the wondrous method of heaven and earth to try to expand the world within the Spiritual River and integrate the power of stars. Water is the source of life, and it can also reflect images, so it is the most suitable.”

Ji Yuan’s explanation was simple, and the Old Dragon quite agreed with his approach. However, the key was in the change in mindset, and the mastery of the wondrous method of heaven and earth. This could already be considered a combination of Cloud Mountain Temple’s star chart and the cultivation manual, all working together to transform the spiritual energy and power of the stars in this area. This was not something that could be easily accomplished.

The effect might not be noticeable in a year or two, or perhaps even for ten or twenty years, but what about a hundred, or several hundred years later? The spiritual environment of the entire Yun Mountain and its surroundings would then be completely different. However, due to the spiritual resonance of the stars, it would be difficult for anyone who did not possess exceptional perception, or who did not cultivate the wondrous method of heaven and earth, to sense these extraordinary changes.

“Haha, although it is gentle and slow, that is precisely why it is more effective and lasting. It is a method that can change the world, yet Mr. Ji speaks of it so lightly!”

The Old Dragon exclaimed in admiration, watching Qin Zizhou also leave his Spiritual River and walk step by step towards them.

However, Ji Yuan felt unworthy of the Old Dragon’s praise — or perhaps he didn’t dare to accept it at all.

“Mr. Ying, please don’t say that. Even the original Water Element Gathering Array, if it were to be maintained for a hundred or several hundred years, would be enough to form a true long-lasting spiritual river. Wouldn’t that also be considered sacred ground?”

The Old Dragon retorted.

“There are quite a few spiritual sacred sites in the world, so what is there to compare them to? But Star Rivers are rare. Who knows, once this place is established, there might only ever be one such river.”

“See, it’s only because it’s rare!”

“Alright, alright, you two, stop arguing about these issues. Regardless, it’s all about transforming the world!”

Qin Zizhou quickly stepped in to smooth things over.

A moment later, the spiritual river gradually disappeared, turning into a thin night mist that dispersed into the surrounding area. It would occasionally emerge as mist again, and since Yun Mountain was never short of clouds and mist so no one would pay much attention to it.

The master and disciple, Taoist Qingsong and Taoist Qingyuan, were still fantasizing about what the mountain gate of Cloud Mountain Temple would be like, and whether it could become a sacred land in the future. Little did they know that the three big shots had already started planning.

Leaving aside otherworldly paradises, sacred lands and spiritual sites were not created overnight. Countless immortal abodes and heavenly sects had accumulated the efforts of generations of successors to reach where they were today. Although the cultivation environment was only one aspect of this effort, it still spoke volumes.

Only after all this was complete could the matter at Cloud Mountain Temple truly be considered closed.

When they returned to Cloud Mountain Temple, it was late at night, and Qi Xuan and Qi Wen were fast asleep. The three did not wake them. Qin Zizhou continued to cultivate in the temple, while Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon bid farewell and left. Qin Zizhou stood in the main hall of the temple, bowing with utmost respect as they ascended to the heavens on clouds.

A short while later, high in the sky outside Yun Mountain, Ji Yuan and the Old Dragon briefly paused. The latter asked Ji Yuan a question.

“What are your plans after this, Mr. Ji? Do you intend to return to your home in Ji Province for a while, or do you plan to visit me?”

Ji Yuan had intended to mention the matter of Yuexiu Island, but considering that it was someone else’s business, and knowing the Old Dragons temperament well enough to know that he wasn’t the kind of person who would act irrationally, he refrained from bringing it up. Instead, he truthfully told the Old Dragon his future plans.

“I might return to Ju’an Pavilion for a while, but I need to visit Yuhuai Mountain. We might travel together to Heng Province in the Northern Territories. If the time is right, I will also visit an old friend.”

The Old Dragon looked slightly puzzled.

“The Northern Territories? What for?”

“Has Mr. Ying heard of Nine Peaks Mountain’s Cultivator Grand Assembly?”

The Old Dragon thought for a moment.

“Oh, so it has been held at Nine Peaks Mountain these past sixty years.”

“That’s right. Interested in joining in the fun?”

The Old Dragon grinned.

“Haha, why would a monster like me go to the Immortal Conference and make a scene? Forget it, go if you want, I have my own things to do.”

Ji Yuan grew serious.

“Is this about that happened with that lustful flood dragon?”

“That’s part of of it. However, the monster races are too diverse, and this matter is related to the Black Wilderness. It all depends on that lizard that you and I deliberately let go.”

With that, the Old Dragon cupped his hands in a respectful gesture towards Ji Yuan.

“In that case, let us part ways here. This trip to Yun Mountain has certainly broadened my horizons and relaxed my mind. I will visit you again another day!”

Ji Yuan returned the salute.

“Or I can visit the Tongtian River sometime!”

“Farewell!”

“Goodbye!”

The Old Dragon took the lead, transforming into a dragon-shaped phantom and disappearing amidst the wind and clouds. Ji Yuan stood on the cloud for a while before turning around and heading towards Ji Province.

While traveling through the sky, Ji Yuan also took a moment to observe the chess pieces scattered as stars in the sky within his inner world. Some of these pieces were illusory and some were real, some were bright and some were dim. Among them, the two representing Cloud Mountain Temple were the most dazzling.

These two chess pieces did belong to Qi Xuan and Qi Wen, but rather one to Qin Zizhou, and the remaining piece represented the entire lineage of Cloud Mountain Temple.

There had been one previous instance where more than one single individual was considered a chess piece: that time, it had been the descendants of the Zuo family, and this time it is Cloud Mountain Temple.

Ji Yuan’s thoughts wandered for a while, weighing what he had learned and gained. Before he knew it, he had returned to Ji Province, and arrived at the outskirts of Ning’an County. It was already late morning.

He descended from the clouds and landed a few li outside the county, walking slowly back to the city. He considered that there were quite a few people in Ning’an County who knew him; after such a long absence, it was best not to suddenly reappear.

However, as it turned out, Ji Yuan was overthinking it. Almost ten years had passed since he last left Ning’an County. Ten years was a short time for cultivators, but for the people living in Ning’an County, ten years was enough for a lot of things to happen.

The eleventh lunar month was already cold, and a slight north wind howled, causing Ji Yuan’s hair to flutter and his clothes to ripple. He walked alone on a road a few miles outside Ning’an County, feeling the still tranquil atmosphere of the place.

The sound of rolling wheels came from behind. A farmer wearing a cotton-padded coat drove a simple canopied oxcart. Seeing the man dressed in rather thin clothes hurrying along, he called out.

“Sir, are you going to Ning’an County? It’s so cold and difficult to walk in this weather. Would you like me to give you a ride?”

“Hey… whoa, whoa…”

The farmer urged the ox to slow down as he asked his questions.

Ji Yuan glanced back at the other man, vaguely noticing that the cart was rather drafty. However, he didn’t mind; he simply wanted to walk around on his own.

“Thank you for your kindness, brother, but I’d like to take a walk by myself.”

“Oh, then I’ll be going now?”

Ji Yuan smiled and cupped his hands in greeting, but did not try to stop him.

The farmer, seeing that the man was refined and his face showed no signs of bearing the cold, realized he wasn’t just putting on a brave face. So he cracked his whip twice on the ox’s rump and continued driving.

About fifteen minutes later, Ji Yuan arrived at Ning’an County. He walked from the city gate to Tian Niu Lane, prepared to be recognized and greeted along the way. Unfortunately, so far no one had recognized him, or even seemed to know him.

The sounds of vendors hawking their wares, chatting, shopkeepers calling out to customers, and other sounds filled the air. Many people looked at Ji Yuan, but none of them regarded him like they would a local. Some of the people in the crowd might have known him from before, but after so many years, even memories of family would be hazy, let alone memories of Ji Yuan.

As Ji Yuan approached Tian Niu Land, he saw the sign for Sun’s Noodle Stall and his spirits lifted. The stall was still in the same spot, its aroma unchanged, familiar even in his blurry vision.

Ji Yuan quickened his pace and soon arrived at the stall. Several customers were already eating noodles. He asked them what was going on as he approached.

“Does the shop still have braised noodles?”

Sun Fu, with graying temples, was tidying up the tableware when he heard the voice. He looked up and saw a refined gentleman, so he quickly replied.

“Yes, yes, yes, we have braised noodles and lamb offal.”

Ji Yuan smiled and sat down in his usual spot.

“Alright, give me a bowl of braised noodles and a bowl of offal soup.”

“Okay, right away!”

Ji Yuan let out a soft breath of relief, and glanced at the diners around him. Some of them were also watching him, and when they saw him looking back, they subconsciously buried their heads back in their noodles.

The diners were mainly drawn to the elegant Mr. Ji due to the exquisite black jade hairpin on his head; even those who didn’t know much about jade would recognize its considerable value.

Beside the noodle stall was a little girl, about seven or eight years old, playing with a dry twig, swinging it back and forth. When she saw Ji Yuan looking over, the little girl immediately stopped swinging the branch and pretended to be quiet.

“Little girl, what’s your name?”

Sun Fu glanced at the little girl and smiled back at Ji Yuan.

“Sir, her name is Sun Yaya, she is my granddaughter.”

“Oh, your granddaughter…”

Ji Yuan frowned slightly, flicked his sleeve, and secretly calculated some divinations with his right hand hidden in his sleeve. He knew that Old Man Sun had already passed away. Lu Shanjun had known about this event back then, but he hadn’t mentioned it to Ji Yuan.

“Sigh…”

“Your noodles and offal are ready, sir!”

Sun Fu carried the tray and brought the food to Ji Yuan. Seeing Ji Yuan sigh, he asked a question.

“Sir, you must be from out of town. Why are you sighing?”

Ji Yuan thanked him and replied.

“It’s nothing, I was just thinking about an old friend.”

With that, he picked up his chopsticks and began to eat. The noodles and offal tasted almost exactly the same as the ones Old Man Sun used to make.

He remembered when Sun Fu had just taken over the noodle stall from his father, the noodles he made were not quite up to par. Now he had truly mastered the craft.

“Not bad, it tastes just like before!”

Sun Fu just said “Oh” and was about to turn away when Ji Yuan’s words caught his attention again.

“Uh, sir, have you eaten my noodles before?”

Sun Fu took a closer look at Ji Yuan. He had initially thought Ji Yuan was around thirty years old, but upon closer inspection, he suddenly found it difficult to discern his age. Ji Yuan had a refined appearance, but he gave off the feeling of an older man, making it hard to pinpoint his age.

“Haha, yes, I have, and I have also eaten your father’s noodles.”

Ji Yuan replied, then continued to eat his noodles with relish. Sun Fu frowned, deep in thought, glancing at Ji Yuan from time to time.

He was still puzzled as he returned to the front of the cart. Then he noticed a wooden sign hanging on the cart’s door with ink-stained characters that read, “Keep one portion.”

In an instant, Sun Fu suddenly realized something. He looked up at Ji Yuan, and blurted out in disbelief.

“You, you’re Mr. Ji!?”

T/n: Happy 400th chapter! 😀

Heads up that I messed up the publication date of ch. 399. It should be fixed now but it was out of order for a bit. Sorry about that, everyone!

Ok, so to clear up some confusion about the Spiritual River stuff they were discussing at the beginning, here’s my take on it: JY modified a Water Elemental Essence gathering array from the Old Dragon to work as both a Water Element array and also a star-power element array. He did this by using the fact that starlight can be reflected on water, and thus collected in the array. Over time, this will also transform the surrounding landscape of Yun Mountain to be attuned towards the heavens and towards celestial power, making it a kind of sacred ground for cultivators who focus in that power.
